===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_TAX:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_TAX(p_country_id INTEGER, p_amount DECIMAL(16,2))
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,2)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_rate DECIMAL(6,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(rate),0) INTO v_rate FROM ERP_TAX_RATE WHERE country_id = p_country_id;
    RETURN COALESCE(p_amount,0) * v_rate;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_DISCOUNT_PCT:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_DISCOUNT_PCT(p_tier VARCHAR(10), p_qty INTEGER)
RETURNS DECIMAL(6,4)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_pct DECIMAL(6,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(pct),0) INTO v_pct FROM ERP_DISCOUNT
    WHERE tier = p_tier AND min_qty <= p_qty;
    RETURN v_pct;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_LINE_TOTAL:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_LINE_TOTAL(p_qty DECIMAL(16,4), p_price DECIMAL(14,2), p_disc DECIMAL(6,4))
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,2)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    RETURN CAST(p_qty * p_price * (1 - COALESCE(p_disc,0)) AS DECIMAL(16,2));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(p_from INTEGER, p_to INTEGER, p_amount DECIMAL(16,2))
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,2)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_rate DECIMAL(16,6) DEFAULT 1;
    IF p_from = p_to THEN RETURN p_amount; END IF;
    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(rate),1) INTO v_rate FROM ERP_EXCHANGE_RATE
    WHERE from_ccy = p_from AND to_ccy = p_to;
    RETURN CAST(COALESCE(p_amount,0) * v_rate AS DECIMAL(16,2));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_ORDER_SUBTOTAL:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_ORDER_SUBTOTAL(p_so_id INTEGER)
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,2)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_total DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(ERP_FN_LINE_TOTAL(qty,unit_price,discount_pct)),0)
    INTO v_total FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE WHERE so_id = p_so_id;
    RETURN v_total;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_MARGIN:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_MARGIN(p_product_id INTEGER)
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,4)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_price DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_cost DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(list_price,0), COALESCE(std_cost,0)
    INTO v_price, v_cost FROM ERP_PRODUCT WHERE id = p_product_id;
    IF v_price = 0 THEN RETURN 0; END IF;
    RETURN CAST((v_price - v_cost) / v_price AS DECIMAL(16,4));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(p_product_id INTEGER)
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,4)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_qty DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(on_hand - reserved),0) INTO v_qty
    FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE product_id = p_product_id;
    RETURN v_qty;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_CREDIT_OK:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_CREDIT_OK(p_customer_id INTEGER, p_amount DECIMAL(16,2))
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_limit DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_bal DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(credit_limit,0), COALESCE(balance,0)
    INTO v_limit, v_bal FROM ERP_CUSTOMER WHERE id = p_customer_id;
    IF (v_bal + COALESCE(p_amount,0)) <= v_limit THEN RETURN 1; ELSE RETURN 0; END IF;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_BOM_COST:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_BOM_COST(p_product_id INTEGER)
RETURNS DECIMAL(18,4)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_cost DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(b.qty_per * rm.unit_cost),0) INTO v_cost
    FROM ERP_BOM b
    JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
    WHERE b.product_id = p_product_id;
    RETURN v_cost;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_REORDER_QTY:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_REORDER_QTY(p_product_id INTEGER, p_target DECIMAL(16,4))
RETURNS DECIMAL(16,4)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_avail DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SET v_avail = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(p_product_id);
    IF v_avail >= p_target THEN RETURN 0; END IF;
    RETURN CAST(p_target - v_avail AS DECIMAL(16,4));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_FN_CUSTOMER_BALANCE:FUNCTION===
CREATE FUNCTION ERP_FN_CUSTOMER_BALANCE(p_customer_id INTEGER)
RETURNS DECIMAL(18,2)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_inv DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_paid DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(i.amount + i.tax),0) INTO v_inv
    FROM ERP_INVOICE i
    JOIN ERP_SALES_ORDER so ON so.id = i.so_id
    WHERE so.customer_id = p_customer_id;
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0) INTO v_paid
    FROM ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa
    JOIN ERP_PAYMENT p ON p.id = pa.payment_id
    WHERE p.customer_id = p_customer_id;
    RETURN v_inv - v_paid;
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_AUDIT:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_AUDIT(IN p_entity VARCHAR(40), IN p_id INTEGER, IN p_action VARCHAR(20), IN p_detail VARCHAR(200))
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
    VALUES (p_entity, p_id, p_action, p_detail, CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_APPLY_PAYMENT:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_APPLY_PAYMENT(IN p_invoice_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_cust INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_amount DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_pay_id INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_bal DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT so.customer_id, COALESCE(i.amount + i.tax,0)
      INTO v_cust, v_amount
    FROM ERP_INVOICE i JOIN ERP_SALES_ORDER so ON so.id = i.so_id
    WHERE i.id = p_invoice_id;
    INSERT INTO ERP_PAYMENT(customer_id, pay_date, amount, method)
    VALUES (v_cust, CURRENT DATE, v_amount, 'AUTO');
    SET v_pay_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
    INSERT INTO ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC(payment_id, invoice_id, amount)
    VALUES (v_pay_id, p_invoice_id, v_amount);
    UPDATE ERP_INVOICE SET status = 'PAID' WHERE id = p_invoice_id;
    UPDATE ERP_CUSTOMER SET balance = COALESCE(balance,0) - v_amount WHERE id = v_cust;
    SET v_bal = ERP_FN_CUSTOMER_BALANCE(v_cust);
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [ap00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_ap00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_ap00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_ap00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_ap00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_ap00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_ap00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_ap00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_ap00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_ap00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap00 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap00;
        loop_ap00: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap00 INTO v_so_ap00, v_cust_ap00, v_ctry_ap00, v_ccy_ap00, v_sub_ap00;
            IF v_done_ap00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_ap00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_ap00, v_sub_ap00);
            SET v_fx_ap00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_ap00, 1, v_sub_ap00 + v_tax_ap00);
            SET v_acc_ap00 = v_acc_ap00 + v_fx_ap00;
            SET v_cnt_ap00 = v_cnt_ap00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap00;
        IF v_cnt_ap00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_ap00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_ap00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [ap01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_ap01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_ap01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_ap01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_ap01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_ap01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap01 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap01;
        loop_ap01: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap01 INTO v_pid_ap01, v_oh_ap01, v_cost_ap01;
            IF v_done_ap01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_ap01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_ap01);
            SET v_val_ap01 = v_val_ap01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_ap01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_ap01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_ap01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_ap01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_ap01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [ap02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_ap02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_ap02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_ap02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_ap02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_ap02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_ap02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap02 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap02;
        loop_ap02: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap02 INTO v_prod_ap02, v_rm_ap02, v_supp_ap02, v_qper_ap02, v_uc_ap02;
            IF v_done_ap02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_ap02 = v_roll_ap02 + COALESCE(v_qper_ap02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_ap02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_ap02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [ap03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_ap03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_ap03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_ap03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_ap03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap03 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap03;
        loop_ap03: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap03 INTO v_acct_ap03, v_deb_ap03, v_cred_ap03;
            IF v_done_ap03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap03; END IF;
            SET v_net_ap03 = v_net_ap03 + (v_deb_ap03 - v_cred_ap03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_ap03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [ap04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_ap04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_ap04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_ap04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_ap04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_ap04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap04 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap04;
        loop_ap04: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap04 INTO v_pid_ap04, v_lp_ap04, v_pp_ap04;
            IF v_done_ap04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap04; END IF;
            SET v_var_ap04 = v_var_ap04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_ap04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_ap04,0));
            SET v_marg_ap04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_ap04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_ap04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [ap05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_ap05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_ap05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_ap05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_ap05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap05 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap05;
        loop_ap05: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap05 INTO v_inv_ap05, v_amt_ap05, v_alloc_ap05;
            IF v_done_ap05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap05; END IF;
            SET v_open_ap05 = v_open_ap05 + (v_amt_ap05 - v_alloc_ap05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_ap05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [ap06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_ap06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_ap06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_ap06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap06 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap06;
        loop_ap06: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap06 INTO v_dept_ap06, v_lvl_ap06, v_head_ap06;
            IF v_done_ap06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_ap06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_ap06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [ap07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_ap07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_ap07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_ap07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_ap07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap07 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap07;
        loop_ap07: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap07 INTO v_sh_ap07, v_car_ap07, v_qty_ap07;
            IF v_done_ap07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_ap07 = v_tot_ap07 + v_qty_ap07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_ap07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [ap08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_ap08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_ap08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_ap08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_ap08 FROM v_sql_ap08;
        EXECUTE st_ap08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_ap08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_ap08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [ap09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_ap09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_ap09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_ap09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_ap09 FROM v_sql_ap09;
        EXECUTE st_ap09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_ap09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_ap09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [ap10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_ap10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_ap10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_ap10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_ap10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [ap11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_ap11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_ap11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_ap11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_ap11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [ap12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_ap12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_ap12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_ap12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_ap12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_ap12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_ap12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_ap12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_ap12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_ap12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap12 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap12;
        loop_ap12: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap12 INTO v_so_ap12, v_cust_ap12, v_ctry_ap12, v_ccy_ap12, v_sub_ap12;
            IF v_done_ap12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_ap12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_ap12, v_sub_ap12);
            SET v_fx_ap12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_ap12, 1, v_sub_ap12 + v_tax_ap12);
            SET v_acc_ap12 = v_acc_ap12 + v_fx_ap12;
            SET v_cnt_ap12 = v_cnt_ap12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap12;
        IF v_cnt_ap12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_ap12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_ap12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [ap13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_ap13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_ap13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_ap13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_ap13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_ap13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap13 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap13;
        loop_ap13: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap13 INTO v_pid_ap13, v_oh_ap13, v_cost_ap13;
            IF v_done_ap13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_ap13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_ap13);
            SET v_val_ap13 = v_val_ap13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_ap13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_ap13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_ap13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_ap13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_ap13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [ap14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_ap14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_ap14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_ap14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_ap14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_ap14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_ap14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ap14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ap14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ap14 = 1;
        OPEN c_ap14;
        loop_ap14: LOOP
            FETCH c_ap14 INTO v_prod_ap14, v_rm_ap14, v_supp_ap14, v_qper_ap14, v_uc_ap14;
            IF v_done_ap14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ap14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_ap14 = v_roll_ap14 + COALESCE(v_qper_ap14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_ap14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ap14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_ap14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('PAYMENT', v_pay_id, 'APPLY',
        'invoice=' || RTRIM(CHAR(p_invoice_id)) || ' bal=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_bal)));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_POST_GL:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_POST_GL(IN p_invoice_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_amount DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_tax DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_ar INTEGER; DECLARE v_rev INTEGER; DECLARE v_taxacct INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_sql VARCHAR(500);
    SELECT COALESCE(amount,0), COALESCE(tax,0) INTO v_amount, v_tax
    FROM ERP_INVOICE WHERE id = p_invoice_id;
    SELECT MIN(id) INTO v_ar FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT WHERE code = '1200';
    SELECT MIN(id) INTO v_rev FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT WHERE code = '4000';
    SELECT MIN(id) INTO v_taxacct FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT WHERE code = '2200';
    INSERT INTO ERP_GL_ENTRY(account_id, entry_date, debit, credit, ref_doc, memo)
    VALUES (v_ar, CURRENT DATE, v_amount + v_tax, 0, p_invoice_id, 'AR for invoice');
    SET v_sql = 'INSERT INTO ERP_GL_ENTRY(account_id, entry_date, debit, credit, ref_doc, memo) VALUES (?, CURRENT DATE, 0, ?, ?, ?)';
    PREPARE gstmt FROM v_sql;
    EXECUTE gstmt USING v_rev, v_amount, p_invoice_id, 'Revenue';
    EXECUTE gstmt USING v_taxacct, v_tax, p_invoice_id, 'Tax payable';
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [pg00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_pg00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_pg00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_pg00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_pg00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_pg00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_pg00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_pg00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_pg00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_pg00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg00 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg00;
        loop_pg00: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg00 INTO v_so_pg00, v_cust_pg00, v_ctry_pg00, v_ccy_pg00, v_sub_pg00;
            IF v_done_pg00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_pg00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_pg00, v_sub_pg00);
            SET v_fx_pg00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_pg00, 1, v_sub_pg00 + v_tax_pg00);
            SET v_acc_pg00 = v_acc_pg00 + v_fx_pg00;
            SET v_cnt_pg00 = v_cnt_pg00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg00;
        IF v_cnt_pg00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_pg00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_pg00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [pg01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pg01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_pg01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_pg01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_pg01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_pg01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg01 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg01;
        loop_pg01: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg01 INTO v_pid_pg01, v_oh_pg01, v_cost_pg01;
            IF v_done_pg01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_pg01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_pg01);
            SET v_val_pg01 = v_val_pg01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_pg01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_pg01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_pg01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_pg01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_pg01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [pg02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_pg02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_pg02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_pg02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_pg02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_pg02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_pg02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg02 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg02;
        loop_pg02: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg02 INTO v_prod_pg02, v_rm_pg02, v_supp_pg02, v_qper_pg02, v_uc_pg02;
            IF v_done_pg02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_pg02 = v_roll_pg02 + COALESCE(v_qper_pg02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_pg02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_pg02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [pg03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_pg03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_pg03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_pg03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_pg03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg03 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg03;
        loop_pg03: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg03 INTO v_acct_pg03, v_deb_pg03, v_cred_pg03;
            IF v_done_pg03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg03; END IF;
            SET v_net_pg03 = v_net_pg03 + (v_deb_pg03 - v_cred_pg03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_pg03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [pg04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pg04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_pg04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_pg04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_pg04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_pg04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg04 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg04;
        loop_pg04: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg04 INTO v_pid_pg04, v_lp_pg04, v_pp_pg04;
            IF v_done_pg04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg04; END IF;
            SET v_var_pg04 = v_var_pg04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_pg04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_pg04,0));
            SET v_marg_pg04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_pg04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_pg04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [pg05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_pg05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_pg05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_pg05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_pg05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg05 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg05;
        loop_pg05: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg05 INTO v_inv_pg05, v_amt_pg05, v_alloc_pg05;
            IF v_done_pg05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg05; END IF;
            SET v_open_pg05 = v_open_pg05 + (v_amt_pg05 - v_alloc_pg05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_pg05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [pg06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_pg06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_pg06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_pg06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg06 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg06;
        loop_pg06: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg06 INTO v_dept_pg06, v_lvl_pg06, v_head_pg06;
            IF v_done_pg06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_pg06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_pg06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [pg07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_pg07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_pg07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_pg07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_pg07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg07 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg07;
        loop_pg07: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg07 INTO v_sh_pg07, v_car_pg07, v_qty_pg07;
            IF v_done_pg07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_pg07 = v_tot_pg07 + v_qty_pg07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_pg07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [pg08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_pg08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_pg08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_pg08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_pg08 FROM v_sql_pg08;
        EXECUTE st_pg08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_pg08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_pg08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [pg09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_pg09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_pg09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_pg09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_pg09 FROM v_sql_pg09;
        EXECUTE st_pg09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_pg09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_pg09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [pg10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_pg10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_pg10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_pg10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_pg10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [pg11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_pg11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_pg11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_pg11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_pg11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [pg12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_pg12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_pg12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_pg12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_pg12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_pg12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_pg12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_pg12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_pg12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_pg12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg12 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg12;
        loop_pg12: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg12 INTO v_so_pg12, v_cust_pg12, v_ctry_pg12, v_ccy_pg12, v_sub_pg12;
            IF v_done_pg12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_pg12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_pg12, v_sub_pg12);
            SET v_fx_pg12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_pg12, 1, v_sub_pg12 + v_tax_pg12);
            SET v_acc_pg12 = v_acc_pg12 + v_fx_pg12;
            SET v_cnt_pg12 = v_cnt_pg12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg12;
        IF v_cnt_pg12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_pg12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_pg12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [pg13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pg13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_pg13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_pg13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_pg13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_pg13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg13 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg13;
        loop_pg13: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg13 INTO v_pid_pg13, v_oh_pg13, v_cost_pg13;
            IF v_done_pg13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_pg13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_pg13);
            SET v_val_pg13 = v_val_pg13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_pg13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_pg13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_pg13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_pg13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_pg13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [pg14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_pg14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_pg14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_pg14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_pg14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_pg14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_pg14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pg14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pg14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pg14 = 1;
        OPEN c_pg14;
        loop_pg14: LOOP
            FETCH c_pg14 INTO v_prod_pg14, v_rm_pg14, v_supp_pg14, v_qper_pg14, v_uc_pg14;
            IF v_done_pg14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pg14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_pg14 = v_roll_pg14 + COALESCE(v_qper_pg14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_pg14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pg14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_pg14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_APPLY_PAYMENT(p_invoice_id);
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('INVOICE', p_invoice_id, 'POST_GL', 'GL posted');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_CREATE_INVOICE:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_CREATE_INVOICE(IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_amount DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_tax DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_country INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_inv_no INTEGER;
    DECLARE p_invoice_id INTEGER;
    SET v_amount = ERP_FN_ORDER_SUBTOTAL(p_so_id);
    SELECT c.country_id INTO v_country
    FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
    WHERE so.id = p_so_id;
    SET v_tax = ERP_FN_TAX(v_country, v_amount);
    SET v_inv_no = NEXT VALUE FOR ERP_INVOICE_SEQ;
    INSERT INTO ERP_INVOICE(so_id, inv_no, inv_date, amount, tax, status)
    VALUES (p_so_id, v_inv_no, CURRENT DATE, v_amount, v_tax, 'OPEN');
    SET p_invoice_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
    INSERT INTO ERP_INVOICE_LINE(invoice_id, so_line_id, amount)
    SELECT p_invoice_id, sol.id, sol.line_total
    FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE sol WHERE sol.so_id = p_so_id;
    UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = 'INVOICED',
        subtotal = v_amount, tax_total = v_tax, grand_total = v_amount + v_tax
    WHERE id = p_so_id;
    UPDATE ERP_CUSTOMER SET balance = COALESCE(balance,0) + v_amount + v_tax
    WHERE id = (SELECT customer_id FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE id = p_so_id);
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [ci00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_ci00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_ci00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_ci00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_ci00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_ci00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_ci00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_ci00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_ci00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_ci00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci00 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci00;
        loop_ci00: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci00 INTO v_so_ci00, v_cust_ci00, v_ctry_ci00, v_ccy_ci00, v_sub_ci00;
            IF v_done_ci00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_ci00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_ci00, v_sub_ci00);
            SET v_fx_ci00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_ci00, 1, v_sub_ci00 + v_tax_ci00);
            SET v_acc_ci00 = v_acc_ci00 + v_fx_ci00;
            SET v_cnt_ci00 = v_cnt_ci00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci00;
        IF v_cnt_ci00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_ci00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_ci00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [ci01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_ci01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_ci01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_ci01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_ci01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_ci01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci01 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci01;
        loop_ci01: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci01 INTO v_pid_ci01, v_oh_ci01, v_cost_ci01;
            IF v_done_ci01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_ci01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_ci01);
            SET v_val_ci01 = v_val_ci01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_ci01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_ci01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_ci01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_ci01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_ci01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [ci02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_ci02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_ci02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_ci02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_ci02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_ci02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_ci02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci02 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci02;
        loop_ci02: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci02 INTO v_prod_ci02, v_rm_ci02, v_supp_ci02, v_qper_ci02, v_uc_ci02;
            IF v_done_ci02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_ci02 = v_roll_ci02 + COALESCE(v_qper_ci02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_ci02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_ci02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [ci03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_ci03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_ci03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_ci03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_ci03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci03 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci03;
        loop_ci03: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci03 INTO v_acct_ci03, v_deb_ci03, v_cred_ci03;
            IF v_done_ci03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci03; END IF;
            SET v_net_ci03 = v_net_ci03 + (v_deb_ci03 - v_cred_ci03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_ci03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [ci04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_ci04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_ci04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_ci04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_ci04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_ci04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci04 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci04;
        loop_ci04: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci04 INTO v_pid_ci04, v_lp_ci04, v_pp_ci04;
            IF v_done_ci04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci04; END IF;
            SET v_var_ci04 = v_var_ci04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_ci04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_ci04,0));
            SET v_marg_ci04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_ci04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_ci04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [ci05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_ci05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_ci05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_ci05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_ci05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci05 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci05;
        loop_ci05: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci05 INTO v_inv_ci05, v_amt_ci05, v_alloc_ci05;
            IF v_done_ci05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci05; END IF;
            SET v_open_ci05 = v_open_ci05 + (v_amt_ci05 - v_alloc_ci05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_ci05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [ci06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_ci06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_ci06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_ci06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci06 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci06;
        loop_ci06: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci06 INTO v_dept_ci06, v_lvl_ci06, v_head_ci06;
            IF v_done_ci06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_ci06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_ci06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [ci07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_ci07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_ci07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_ci07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_ci07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci07 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci07;
        loop_ci07: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci07 INTO v_sh_ci07, v_car_ci07, v_qty_ci07;
            IF v_done_ci07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_ci07 = v_tot_ci07 + v_qty_ci07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_ci07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [ci08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_ci08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_ci08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_ci08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_ci08 FROM v_sql_ci08;
        EXECUTE st_ci08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_ci08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_ci08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [ci09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_ci09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_ci09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_ci09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_ci09 FROM v_sql_ci09;
        EXECUTE st_ci09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_ci09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_ci09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [ci10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_ci10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_ci10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_ci10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_ci10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [ci11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_ci11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_ci11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_ci11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_ci11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [ci12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_ci12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_ci12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_ci12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_ci12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_ci12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_ci12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_ci12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_ci12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_ci12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci12 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci12;
        loop_ci12: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci12 INTO v_so_ci12, v_cust_ci12, v_ctry_ci12, v_ccy_ci12, v_sub_ci12;
            IF v_done_ci12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_ci12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_ci12, v_sub_ci12);
            SET v_fx_ci12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_ci12, 1, v_sub_ci12 + v_tax_ci12);
            SET v_acc_ci12 = v_acc_ci12 + v_fx_ci12;
            SET v_cnt_ci12 = v_cnt_ci12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci12;
        IF v_cnt_ci12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_ci12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_ci12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [ci13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_ci13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_ci13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_ci13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_ci13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_ci13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci13 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci13;
        loop_ci13: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci13 INTO v_pid_ci13, v_oh_ci13, v_cost_ci13;
            IF v_done_ci13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_ci13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_ci13);
            SET v_val_ci13 = v_val_ci13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_ci13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_ci13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_ci13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_ci13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_ci13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [ci14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_ci14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_ci14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_ci14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_ci14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_ci14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_ci14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci14 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci14;
        loop_ci14: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci14 INTO v_prod_ci14, v_rm_ci14, v_supp_ci14, v_qper_ci14, v_uc_ci14;
            IF v_done_ci14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_ci14 = v_roll_ci14 + COALESCE(v_qper_ci14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_ci14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_ci14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [ci15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_ci15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_ci15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_ci15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_ci15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_ci15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_ci15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_ci15 = 1;
        OPEN c_ci15;
        loop_ci15: LOOP
            FETCH c_ci15 INTO v_acct_ci15, v_deb_ci15, v_cred_ci15;
            IF v_done_ci15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_ci15; END IF;
            SET v_net_ci15 = v_net_ci15 + (v_deb_ci15 - v_cred_ci15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_ci15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_ci15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_POST_GL(p_invoice_id);
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('INVOICE', p_invoice_id, 'CREATE', 'invoice created');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_CREATE_SHIPMENT:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_CREATE_SHIPMENT(IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_ship_id INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_carrier INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_doc INTEGER;
    SELECT MIN(id) INTO v_carrier FROM ERP_CARRIER;
    SET v_doc = NEXT VALUE FOR ERP_DOC_SEQ;
    INSERT INTO ERP_SHIPMENT(so_id, carrier_id, ship_date, tracking_no, status)
    VALUES (p_so_id, v_carrier, CURRENT DATE, 'TRK' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_doc)), 'SHIPPED');
    SET v_ship_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
    INSERT INTO ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE(shipment_id, so_line_id, qty_shipped)
    SELECT v_ship_id, sol.id, sol.qty
    FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE sol WHERE sol.so_id = p_so_id;
    UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = 'SHIPPED' WHERE id = p_so_id;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [cs00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_cs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_cs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_cs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_cs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_cs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_cs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_cs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_cs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_cs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs00 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs00;
        loop_cs00: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs00 INTO v_so_cs00, v_cust_cs00, v_ctry_cs00, v_ccy_cs00, v_sub_cs00;
            IF v_done_cs00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_cs00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_cs00, v_sub_cs00);
            SET v_fx_cs00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_cs00, 1, v_sub_cs00 + v_tax_cs00);
            SET v_acc_cs00 = v_acc_cs00 + v_fx_cs00;
            SET v_cnt_cs00 = v_cnt_cs00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs00;
        IF v_cnt_cs00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_cs00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_cs00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [cs01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_cs01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_cs01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_cs01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_cs01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_cs01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs01 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs01;
        loop_cs01: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs01 INTO v_pid_cs01, v_oh_cs01, v_cost_cs01;
            IF v_done_cs01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_cs01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_cs01);
            SET v_val_cs01 = v_val_cs01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_cs01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_cs01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_cs01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_cs01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_cs01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [cs02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_cs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_cs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_cs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_cs02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_cs02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_cs02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs02 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs02;
        loop_cs02: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs02 INTO v_prod_cs02, v_rm_cs02, v_supp_cs02, v_qper_cs02, v_uc_cs02;
            IF v_done_cs02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_cs02 = v_roll_cs02 + COALESCE(v_qper_cs02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_cs02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_cs02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [cs03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_cs03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_cs03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_cs03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_cs03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs03 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs03;
        loop_cs03: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs03 INTO v_acct_cs03, v_deb_cs03, v_cred_cs03;
            IF v_done_cs03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs03; END IF;
            SET v_net_cs03 = v_net_cs03 + (v_deb_cs03 - v_cred_cs03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_cs03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [cs04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_cs04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_cs04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_cs04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_cs04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_cs04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs04 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs04;
        loop_cs04: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs04 INTO v_pid_cs04, v_lp_cs04, v_pp_cs04;
            IF v_done_cs04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs04; END IF;
            SET v_var_cs04 = v_var_cs04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_cs04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_cs04,0));
            SET v_marg_cs04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_cs04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_cs04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [cs05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_cs05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_cs05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_cs05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_cs05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs05 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs05;
        loop_cs05: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs05 INTO v_inv_cs05, v_amt_cs05, v_alloc_cs05;
            IF v_done_cs05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs05; END IF;
            SET v_open_cs05 = v_open_cs05 + (v_amt_cs05 - v_alloc_cs05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_cs05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [cs06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_cs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_cs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_cs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs06 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs06;
        loop_cs06: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs06 INTO v_dept_cs06, v_lvl_cs06, v_head_cs06;
            IF v_done_cs06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_cs06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_cs06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [cs07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_cs07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_cs07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_cs07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_cs07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs07 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs07;
        loop_cs07: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs07 INTO v_sh_cs07, v_car_cs07, v_qty_cs07;
            IF v_done_cs07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_cs07 = v_tot_cs07 + v_qty_cs07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_cs07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [cs08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_cs08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_cs08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_cs08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_cs08 FROM v_sql_cs08;
        EXECUTE st_cs08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_cs08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_cs08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [cs09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_cs09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_cs09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_cs09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_cs09 FROM v_sql_cs09;
        EXECUTE st_cs09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_cs09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_cs09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [cs10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_cs10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_cs10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_cs10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_cs10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [cs11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_cs11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_cs11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_cs11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_cs11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [cs12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_cs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_cs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_cs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_cs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_cs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_cs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_cs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_cs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_cs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs12 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs12;
        loop_cs12: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs12 INTO v_so_cs12, v_cust_cs12, v_ctry_cs12, v_ccy_cs12, v_sub_cs12;
            IF v_done_cs12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_cs12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_cs12, v_sub_cs12);
            SET v_fx_cs12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_cs12, 1, v_sub_cs12 + v_tax_cs12);
            SET v_acc_cs12 = v_acc_cs12 + v_fx_cs12;
            SET v_cnt_cs12 = v_cnt_cs12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs12;
        IF v_cnt_cs12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_cs12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_cs12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [cs13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_cs13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_cs13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_cs13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_cs13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_cs13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs13 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs13;
        loop_cs13: LOOP
