            FETCH c_cs13 INTO v_pid_cs13, v_oh_cs13, v_cost_cs13;
            IF v_done_cs13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_cs13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_cs13);
            SET v_val_cs13 = v_val_cs13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_cs13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_cs13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_cs13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_cs13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_cs13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [cs14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_cs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_cs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_cs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_cs14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_cs14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_cs14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs14 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs14;
        loop_cs14: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs14 INTO v_prod_cs14, v_rm_cs14, v_supp_cs14, v_qper_cs14, v_uc_cs14;
            IF v_done_cs14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_cs14 = v_roll_cs14 + COALESCE(v_qper_cs14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_cs14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_cs14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [cs15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_cs15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_cs15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_cs15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_cs15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cs15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cs15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cs15 = 1;
        OPEN c_cs15;
        loop_cs15: LOOP
            FETCH c_cs15 INTO v_acct_cs15, v_deb_cs15, v_cred_cs15;
            IF v_done_cs15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cs15; END IF;
            SET v_net_cs15 = v_net_cs15 + (v_deb_cs15 - v_cred_cs15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cs15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_cs15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_CREATE_INVOICE(p_so_id);
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('SHIPMENT', v_ship_id, 'CREATE', 'shipment created');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_SCHEDULE_OPS:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_SCHEDULE_OPS(IN p_work_order_id INTEGER, IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_dept INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_seq INTEGER DEFAULT 1;
    DECLARE done INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE c_dept CURSOR FOR SELECT id FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT WHERE code IN ('MFG','OPS') ORDER BY id;
    DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = 1;
    OPEN c_dept;
    ops_loop: LOOP
        FETCH c_dept INTO v_dept;
        IF done = 1 THEN LEAVE ops_loop; END IF;
        INSERT INTO ERP_WORK_ORDER_OP(work_order_id, seq_no, op_name, dept_id, status)
        VALUES (p_work_order_id, v_seq, 'OP-' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_seq)), v_dept, 'PLANNED');
        SET v_seq = v_seq + 1;
    END LOOP;
    CLOSE c_dept;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [so00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_so00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_so00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_so00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_so00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_so00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_so00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_so00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_so00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_so00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so00 = 1;
        OPEN c_so00;
        loop_so00: LOOP
            FETCH c_so00 INTO v_so_so00, v_cust_so00, v_ctry_so00, v_ccy_so00, v_sub_so00;
            IF v_done_so00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_so00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_so00, v_sub_so00);
            SET v_fx_so00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_so00, 1, v_sub_so00 + v_tax_so00);
            SET v_acc_so00 = v_acc_so00 + v_fx_so00;
            SET v_cnt_so00 = v_cnt_so00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so00;
        IF v_cnt_so00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_so00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_so00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [so01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_so01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_so01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_so01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_so01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_so01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so01 = 1;
        OPEN c_so01;
        loop_so01: LOOP
            FETCH c_so01 INTO v_pid_so01, v_oh_so01, v_cost_so01;
            IF v_done_so01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_so01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_so01);
            SET v_val_so01 = v_val_so01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_so01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_so01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_so01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_so01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_so01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [so02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_so02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_so02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_so02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_so02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_so02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_so02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so02 = 1;
        OPEN c_so02;
        loop_so02: LOOP
            FETCH c_so02 INTO v_prod_so02, v_rm_so02, v_supp_so02, v_qper_so02, v_uc_so02;
            IF v_done_so02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_so02 = v_roll_so02 + COALESCE(v_qper_so02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_so02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_so02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [so03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_so03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_so03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_so03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_so03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so03 = 1;
        OPEN c_so03;
        loop_so03: LOOP
            FETCH c_so03 INTO v_acct_so03, v_deb_so03, v_cred_so03;
            IF v_done_so03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so03; END IF;
            SET v_net_so03 = v_net_so03 + (v_deb_so03 - v_cred_so03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_so03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [so04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_so04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_so04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_so04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_so04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_so04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so04 = 1;
        OPEN c_so04;
        loop_so04: LOOP
            FETCH c_so04 INTO v_pid_so04, v_lp_so04, v_pp_so04;
            IF v_done_so04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so04; END IF;
            SET v_var_so04 = v_var_so04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_so04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_so04,0));
            SET v_marg_so04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_so04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_so04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [so05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_so05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_so05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_so05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_so05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so05 = 1;
        OPEN c_so05;
        loop_so05: LOOP
            FETCH c_so05 INTO v_inv_so05, v_amt_so05, v_alloc_so05;
            IF v_done_so05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so05; END IF;
            SET v_open_so05 = v_open_so05 + (v_amt_so05 - v_alloc_so05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_so05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [so06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_so06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_so06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_so06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so06 = 1;
        OPEN c_so06;
        loop_so06: LOOP
            FETCH c_so06 INTO v_dept_so06, v_lvl_so06, v_head_so06;
            IF v_done_so06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_so06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_so06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [so07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_so07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_so07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_so07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_so07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so07 = 1;
        OPEN c_so07;
        loop_so07: LOOP
            FETCH c_so07 INTO v_sh_so07, v_car_so07, v_qty_so07;
            IF v_done_so07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_so07 = v_tot_so07 + v_qty_so07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_so07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [so08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_so08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_so08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_so08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_so08 FROM v_sql_so08;
        EXECUTE st_so08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_so08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_so08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [so09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_so09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_so09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_so09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_so09 FROM v_sql_so09;
        EXECUTE st_so09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_so09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_so09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [so10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_so10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_so10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_so10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_so10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [so11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_so11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_so11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_so11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_so11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [so12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_so12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_so12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_so12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_so12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_so12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_so12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_so12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_so12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_so12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so12 = 1;
        OPEN c_so12;
        loop_so12: LOOP
            FETCH c_so12 INTO v_so_so12, v_cust_so12, v_ctry_so12, v_ccy_so12, v_sub_so12;
            IF v_done_so12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_so12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_so12, v_sub_so12);
            SET v_fx_so12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_so12, 1, v_sub_so12 + v_tax_so12);
            SET v_acc_so12 = v_acc_so12 + v_fx_so12;
            SET v_cnt_so12 = v_cnt_so12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so12;
        IF v_cnt_so12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_so12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_so12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [so13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_so13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_so13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_so13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_so13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_so13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so13 = 1;
        OPEN c_so13;
        loop_so13: LOOP
            FETCH c_so13 INTO v_pid_so13, v_oh_so13, v_cost_so13;
            IF v_done_so13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_so13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_so13);
            SET v_val_so13 = v_val_so13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_so13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_so13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_so13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_so13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_so13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [so14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_so14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_so14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_so14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_so14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_so14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_so14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so14 = 1;
        OPEN c_so14;
        loop_so14: LOOP
            FETCH c_so14 INTO v_prod_so14, v_rm_so14, v_supp_so14, v_qper_so14, v_uc_so14;
            IF v_done_so14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_so14 = v_roll_so14 + COALESCE(v_qper_so14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_so14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_so14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [so15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_so15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_so15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_so15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_so15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_so15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_so15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_so15 = 1;
        OPEN c_so15;
        loop_so15: LOOP
            FETCH c_so15 INTO v_acct_so15, v_deb_so15, v_cred_so15;
            IF v_done_so15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_so15; END IF;
            SET v_net_so15 = v_net_so15 + (v_deb_so15 - v_cred_so15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_so15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_so15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_CREATE_SHIPMENT(p_so_id);
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('WORK_ORDER', p_work_order_id, 'SCHEDULE', 'ops scheduled');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_CREATE_WORK_ORDER:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_CREATE_WORK_ORDER(IN p_product_id INTEGER, IN p_qty DECIMAL(16,4), IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_wo_id INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_manufactured SMALLINT DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_bom_cost DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COALESCE(is_manufactured,0) INTO v_manufactured FROM ERP_PRODUCT WHERE id = p_product_id;
    SET v_bom_cost = ERP_FN_BOM_COST(p_product_id);
    IF v_manufactured = 1 THEN
        INSERT INTO ERP_WORK_ORDER(product_id, qty, status, start_date)
        VALUES (p_product_id, p_qty, 'OPEN', CURRENT DATE);
        SET v_wo_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
        CALL ERP_USP_SCHEDULE_OPS(v_wo_id, p_so_id);
    ELSE
        CALL ERP_USP_CREATE_SHIPMENT(p_so_id);
    END IF;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [cw00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_cw00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_cw00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_cw00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_cw00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_cw00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_cw00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_cw00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_cw00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_cw00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw00 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw00;
        loop_cw00: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw00 INTO v_so_cw00, v_cust_cw00, v_ctry_cw00, v_ccy_cw00, v_sub_cw00;
            IF v_done_cw00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_cw00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_cw00, v_sub_cw00);
            SET v_fx_cw00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_cw00, 1, v_sub_cw00 + v_tax_cw00);
            SET v_acc_cw00 = v_acc_cw00 + v_fx_cw00;
            SET v_cnt_cw00 = v_cnt_cw00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw00;
        IF v_cnt_cw00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_cw00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_cw00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [cw01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_cw01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_cw01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_cw01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_cw01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_cw01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw01 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw01;
        loop_cw01: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw01 INTO v_pid_cw01, v_oh_cw01, v_cost_cw01;
            IF v_done_cw01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_cw01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_cw01);
            SET v_val_cw01 = v_val_cw01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_cw01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_cw01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_cw01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_cw01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_cw01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [cw02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_cw02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_cw02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_cw02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_cw02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_cw02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_cw02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw02 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw02;
        loop_cw02: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw02 INTO v_prod_cw02, v_rm_cw02, v_supp_cw02, v_qper_cw02, v_uc_cw02;
            IF v_done_cw02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_cw02 = v_roll_cw02 + COALESCE(v_qper_cw02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_cw02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_cw02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [cw03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_cw03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_cw03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_cw03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_cw03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw03 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw03;
        loop_cw03: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw03 INTO v_acct_cw03, v_deb_cw03, v_cred_cw03;
            IF v_done_cw03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw03; END IF;
            SET v_net_cw03 = v_net_cw03 + (v_deb_cw03 - v_cred_cw03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_cw03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [cw04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_cw04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_cw04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_cw04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_cw04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_cw04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw04 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw04;
        loop_cw04: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw04 INTO v_pid_cw04, v_lp_cw04, v_pp_cw04;
            IF v_done_cw04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw04; END IF;
            SET v_var_cw04 = v_var_cw04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_cw04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_cw04,0));
            SET v_marg_cw04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_cw04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_cw04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [cw05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_cw05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_cw05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_cw05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_cw05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw05 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw05;
        loop_cw05: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw05 INTO v_inv_cw05, v_amt_cw05, v_alloc_cw05;
            IF v_done_cw05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw05; END IF;
            SET v_open_cw05 = v_open_cw05 + (v_amt_cw05 - v_alloc_cw05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_cw05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [cw06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_cw06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_cw06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_cw06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw06 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw06;
        loop_cw06: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw06 INTO v_dept_cw06, v_lvl_cw06, v_head_cw06;
            IF v_done_cw06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_cw06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_cw06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [cw07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_cw07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_cw07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_cw07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_cw07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw07 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw07;
        loop_cw07: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw07 INTO v_sh_cw07, v_car_cw07, v_qty_cw07;
            IF v_done_cw07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_cw07 = v_tot_cw07 + v_qty_cw07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_cw07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [cw08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_cw08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_cw08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_cw08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_cw08 FROM v_sql_cw08;
        EXECUTE st_cw08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_cw08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_cw08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [cw09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_cw09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_cw09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_cw09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_cw09 FROM v_sql_cw09;
        EXECUTE st_cw09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_cw09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_cw09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [cw10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_cw10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_cw10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_cw10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_cw10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [cw11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_cw11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_cw11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_cw11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_cw11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [cw12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_cw12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_cw12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_cw12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_cw12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_cw12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_cw12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_cw12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_cw12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_cw12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw12 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw12;
        loop_cw12: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw12 INTO v_so_cw12, v_cust_cw12, v_ctry_cw12, v_ccy_cw12, v_sub_cw12;
            IF v_done_cw12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_cw12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_cw12, v_sub_cw12);
            SET v_fx_cw12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_cw12, 1, v_sub_cw12 + v_tax_cw12);
            SET v_acc_cw12 = v_acc_cw12 + v_fx_cw12;
            SET v_cnt_cw12 = v_cnt_cw12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw12;
        IF v_cnt_cw12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_cw12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_cw12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [cw13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_cw13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_cw13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_cw13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_cw13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_cw13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw13 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw13;
        loop_cw13: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw13 INTO v_pid_cw13, v_oh_cw13, v_cost_cw13;
            IF v_done_cw13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_cw13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_cw13);
            SET v_val_cw13 = v_val_cw13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_cw13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_cw13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_cw13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_cw13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_cw13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [cw14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_cw14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_cw14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_cw14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_cw14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_cw14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_cw14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw14 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw14;
        loop_cw14: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw14 INTO v_prod_cw14, v_rm_cw14, v_supp_cw14, v_qper_cw14, v_uc_cw14;
            IF v_done_cw14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_cw14 = v_roll_cw14 + COALESCE(v_qper_cw14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_cw14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_cw14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [cw15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_cw15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_cw15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_cw15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_cw15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_cw15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_cw15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_cw15 = 1;
        OPEN c_cw15;
        loop_cw15: LOOP
            FETCH c_cw15 INTO v_acct_cw15, v_deb_cw15, v_cred_cw15;
            IF v_done_cw15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_cw15; END IF;
            SET v_net_cw15 = v_net_cw15 + (v_deb_cw15 - v_cred_cw15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_cw15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_cw15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('WORK_ORDER', COALESCE(v_wo_id,0), 'CREATE',
        'bom_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_bom_cost)));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_PROCURE_MATERIAL:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_PROCURE_MATERIAL(IN p_raw_material_id INTEGER, IN p_qty DECIMAL(16,4))
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_supp INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_po_id INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_pol_id INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_gr_id INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_uc DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_doc INTEGER;
    SELECT supplier_id, COALESCE(unit_cost,0) INTO v_supp, v_uc
    FROM ERP_RAW_MATERIAL WHERE id = p_raw_material_id;
    SET v_doc = NEXT VALUE FOR ERP_DOC_SEQ;
    INSERT INTO ERP_PURCHASE_ORDER(supplier_id, doc_no, order_date, status, total)
    VALUES (v_supp, v_doc, CURRENT DATE, 'OPEN', CAST(p_qty * v_uc AS DECIMAL(16,2)));
    SET v_po_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
    INSERT INTO ERP_PURCHASE_ORDER_LINE(po_id, raw_material_id, qty, unit_cost, line_total)
    VALUES (v_po_id, p_raw_material_id, p_qty, v_uc, CAST(p_qty * v_uc AS DECIMAL(16,2)));
    SET v_pol_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
    INSERT INTO ERP_GOODS_RECEIPT(po_id, receipt_date) VALUES (v_po_id, CURRENT DATE);
    SET v_gr_id = IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL();
    INSERT INTO ERP_GOODS_RECEIPT_LINE(gr_id, po_line_id, qty_received)
    VALUES (v_gr_id, v_pol_id, p_qty);
    UPDATE ERP_PURCHASE_ORDER SET status = 'RECEIVED' WHERE id = v_po_id;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [pm00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_pm00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_pm00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_pm00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_pm00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_pm00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_pm00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_pm00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_pm00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_pm00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm00 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm00;
        loop_pm00: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm00 INTO v_so_pm00, v_cust_pm00, v_ctry_pm00, v_ccy_pm00, v_sub_pm00;
            IF v_done_pm00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_pm00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_pm00, v_sub_pm00);
            SET v_fx_pm00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_pm00, 1, v_sub_pm00 + v_tax_pm00);
            SET v_acc_pm00 = v_acc_pm00 + v_fx_pm00;
            SET v_cnt_pm00 = v_cnt_pm00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm00;
        IF v_cnt_pm00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_pm00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_pm00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [pm01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pm01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_pm01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_pm01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_pm01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_pm01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm01 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm01;
        loop_pm01: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm01 INTO v_pid_pm01, v_oh_pm01, v_cost_pm01;
            IF v_done_pm01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_pm01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_pm01);
            SET v_val_pm01 = v_val_pm01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_pm01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_pm01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_pm01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_pm01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_pm01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [pm02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_pm02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_pm02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_pm02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_pm02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_pm02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_pm02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm02 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm02;
        loop_pm02: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm02 INTO v_prod_pm02, v_rm_pm02, v_supp_pm02, v_qper_pm02, v_uc_pm02;
            IF v_done_pm02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_pm02 = v_roll_pm02 + COALESCE(v_qper_pm02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_pm02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_pm02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [pm03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_pm03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_pm03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_pm03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_pm03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm03 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm03;
        loop_pm03: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm03 INTO v_acct_pm03, v_deb_pm03, v_cred_pm03;
            IF v_done_pm03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm03; END IF;
            SET v_net_pm03 = v_net_pm03 + (v_deb_pm03 - v_cred_pm03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_pm03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [pm04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pm04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_pm04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_pm04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_pm04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_pm04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm04 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm04;
        loop_pm04: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm04 INTO v_pid_pm04, v_lp_pm04, v_pp_pm04;
            IF v_done_pm04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm04; END IF;
            SET v_var_pm04 = v_var_pm04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_pm04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_pm04,0));
            SET v_marg_pm04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_pm04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_pm04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [pm05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_pm05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_pm05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_pm05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_pm05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm05 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm05;
        loop_pm05: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm05 INTO v_inv_pm05, v_amt_pm05, v_alloc_pm05;
            IF v_done_pm05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm05; END IF;
            SET v_open_pm05 = v_open_pm05 + (v_amt_pm05 - v_alloc_pm05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_pm05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [pm06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_pm06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_pm06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_pm06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm06 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm06;
        loop_pm06: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm06 INTO v_dept_pm06, v_lvl_pm06, v_head_pm06;
            IF v_done_pm06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_pm06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_pm06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [pm07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_pm07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_pm07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_pm07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_pm07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm07 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm07;
        loop_pm07: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm07 INTO v_sh_pm07, v_car_pm07, v_qty_pm07;
            IF v_done_pm07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_pm07 = v_tot_pm07 + v_qty_pm07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_pm07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [pm08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_pm08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_pm08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_pm08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_pm08 FROM v_sql_pm08;
        EXECUTE st_pm08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_pm08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_pm08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [pm09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_pm09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_pm09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_pm09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_pm09 FROM v_sql_pm09;
        EXECUTE st_pm09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_pm09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_pm09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [pm10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_pm10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_pm10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_pm10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_pm10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [pm11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_pm11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_pm11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_pm11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_pm11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [pm12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_pm12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_pm12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_pm12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_pm12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_pm12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_pm12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_pm12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_pm12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_pm12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm12 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm12;
        loop_pm12: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm12 INTO v_so_pm12, v_cust_pm12, v_ctry_pm12, v_ccy_pm12, v_sub_pm12;
            IF v_done_pm12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_pm12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_pm12, v_sub_pm12);
            SET v_fx_pm12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_pm12, 1, v_sub_pm12 + v_tax_pm12);
            SET v_acc_pm12 = v_acc_pm12 + v_fx_pm12;
            SET v_cnt_pm12 = v_cnt_pm12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm12;
        IF v_cnt_pm12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_pm12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_pm12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [pm13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pm13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_pm13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_pm13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_pm13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_pm13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm13 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm13;
        loop_pm13: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm13 INTO v_pid_pm13, v_oh_pm13, v_cost_pm13;
            IF v_done_pm13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_pm13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_pm13);
            SET v_val_pm13 = v_val_pm13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_pm13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_pm13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_pm13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_pm13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_pm13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [pm14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_pm14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_pm14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_pm14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_pm14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_pm14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_pm14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pm14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pm14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pm14 = 1;
        OPEN c_pm14;
        loop_pm14: LOOP
            FETCH c_pm14 INTO v_prod_pm14, v_rm_pm14, v_supp_pm14, v_qper_pm14, v_uc_pm14;
            IF v_done_pm14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pm14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_pm14 = v_roll_pm14 + COALESCE(v_qper_pm14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_pm14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pm14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_pm14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('PURCHASE_ORDER', v_po_id, 'PROCURE', 'material procured');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_REPLENISH_STOCK:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_REPLENISH_STOCK(IN p_product_id INTEGER, IN p_qty DECIMAL(16,4), IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_rm INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_qper DECIMAL(14,4);
    DECLARE v_need DECIMAL(16,4);
    DECLARE v_reorder DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE done INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE c_bom CURSOR FOR
        SELECT b.raw_material_id, b.qty_per
        FROM ERP_BOM b
        JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
        WHERE b.product_id = p_product_id
        ORDER BY b.id;
    DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = 1;
    SET v_reorder = ERP_FN_REORDER_QTY(p_product_id, p_qty);
    OPEN c_bom;
    rep_loop: LOOP
        FETCH c_bom INTO v_rm, v_qper;
        IF done = 1 THEN LEAVE rep_loop; END IF;
        SET v_need = COALESCE(v_qper,0) * p_qty;
        IF v_need > 0 THEN
            CALL ERP_USP_PROCURE_MATERIAL(v_rm, v_need);
        END IF;
    END LOOP;
    CLOSE c_bom;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [rp00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_rp00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_rp00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_rp00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_rp00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_rp00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_rp00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_rp00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_rp00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_rp00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp00 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp00;
        loop_rp00: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp00 INTO v_so_rp00, v_cust_rp00, v_ctry_rp00, v_ccy_rp00, v_sub_rp00;
            IF v_done_rp00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_rp00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_rp00, v_sub_rp00);
            SET v_fx_rp00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_rp00, 1, v_sub_rp00 + v_tax_rp00);
            SET v_acc_rp00 = v_acc_rp00 + v_fx_rp00;
            SET v_cnt_rp00 = v_cnt_rp00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp00;
        IF v_cnt_rp00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_rp00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_rp00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [rp01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rp01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_rp01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_rp01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_rp01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_rp01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp01 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp01;
        loop_rp01: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp01 INTO v_pid_rp01, v_oh_rp01, v_cost_rp01;
            IF v_done_rp01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_rp01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_rp01);
            SET v_val_rp01 = v_val_rp01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_rp01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_rp01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_rp01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_rp01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_rp01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [rp02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_rp02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_rp02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_rp02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_rp02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_rp02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_rp02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp02 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp02;
        loop_rp02: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp02 INTO v_prod_rp02, v_rm_rp02, v_supp_rp02, v_qper_rp02, v_uc_rp02;
            IF v_done_rp02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_rp02 = v_roll_rp02 + COALESCE(v_qper_rp02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_rp02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_rp02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [rp03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_rp03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_rp03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_rp03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_rp03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp03 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp03;
        loop_rp03: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp03 INTO v_acct_rp03, v_deb_rp03, v_cred_rp03;
            IF v_done_rp03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp03; END IF;
            SET v_net_rp03 = v_net_rp03 + (v_deb_rp03 - v_cred_rp03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_rp03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [rp04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rp04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_rp04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_rp04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_rp04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_rp04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp04 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp04;
        loop_rp04: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp04 INTO v_pid_rp04, v_lp_rp04, v_pp_rp04;
            IF v_done_rp04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp04; END IF;
            SET v_var_rp04 = v_var_rp04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_rp04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_rp04,0));
            SET v_marg_rp04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_rp04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_rp04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [rp05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_rp05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_rp05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_rp05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_rp05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp05 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp05;
        loop_rp05: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp05 INTO v_inv_rp05, v_amt_rp05, v_alloc_rp05;
            IF v_done_rp05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp05; END IF;
            SET v_open_rp05 = v_open_rp05 + (v_amt_rp05 - v_alloc_rp05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_rp05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [rp06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_rp06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_rp06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_rp06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp06 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp06;
        loop_rp06: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp06 INTO v_dept_rp06, v_lvl_rp06, v_head_rp06;
            IF v_done_rp06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_rp06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_rp06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [rp07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_rp07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_rp07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_rp07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_rp07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp07 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp07;
        loop_rp07: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp07 INTO v_sh_rp07, v_car_rp07, v_qty_rp07;
            IF v_done_rp07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_rp07 = v_tot_rp07 + v_qty_rp07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_rp07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [rp08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_rp08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_rp08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_rp08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_rp08 FROM v_sql_rp08;
        EXECUTE st_rp08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_rp08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_rp08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [rp09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_rp09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_rp09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_rp09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_rp09 FROM v_sql_rp09;
        EXECUTE st_rp09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_rp09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_rp09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [rp10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_rp10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_rp10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_rp10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_rp10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [rp11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_rp11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_rp11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_rp11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_rp11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [rp12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_rp12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_rp12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_rp12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_rp12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_rp12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_rp12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_rp12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_rp12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_rp12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp12 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp12;
        loop_rp12: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp12 INTO v_so_rp12, v_cust_rp12, v_ctry_rp12, v_ccy_rp12, v_sub_rp12;
            IF v_done_rp12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_rp12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_rp12, v_sub_rp12);
            SET v_fx_rp12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_rp12, 1, v_sub_rp12 + v_tax_rp12);
            SET v_acc_rp12 = v_acc_rp12 + v_fx_rp12;
            SET v_cnt_rp12 = v_cnt_rp12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp12;
        IF v_cnt_rp12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_rp12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_rp12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [rp13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rp13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_rp13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_rp13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_rp13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_rp13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp13 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp13;
        loop_rp13: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp13 INTO v_pid_rp13, v_oh_rp13, v_cost_rp13;
            IF v_done_rp13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_rp13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_rp13);
            SET v_val_rp13 = v_val_rp13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_rp13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_rp13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_rp13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_rp13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_rp13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;
