    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [rp14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_rp14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_rp14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_rp14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_rp14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_rp14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_rp14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rp14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rp14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rp14 = 1;
        OPEN c_rp14;
        loop_rp14: LOOP
            FETCH c_rp14 INTO v_prod_rp14, v_rm_rp14, v_supp_rp14, v_qper_rp14, v_uc_rp14;
            IF v_done_rp14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rp14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_rp14 = v_roll_rp14 + COALESCE(v_qper_rp14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_rp14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rp14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_rp14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_CREATE_WORK_ORDER(p_product_id, p_qty, p_so_id);
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('PRODUCT', p_product_id, 'REPLENISH',
        'reorder=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_reorder)));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_RESERVE_STOCK:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_RESERVE_STOCK(IN p_product_id INTEGER, IN p_qty DECIMAL(16,4), IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_inv_id INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_avail DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT MIN(id) INTO v_inv_id FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE product_id = p_product_id;
    SET v_avail = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(p_product_id);
    MERGE INTO ERP_INVENTORY AS tgt
    USING (VALUES (v_inv_id, p_qty)) AS src(inv_id, q)
    ON tgt.id = src.inv_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET reserved = COALESCE(tgt.reserved,0) + src.q
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (product_id, location_id, on_hand, reserved)
        VALUES (p_product_id, (SELECT MIN(id) FROM ERP_LOCATION), 0, p_qty);
    INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
    VALUES (v_inv_id, 'RESERVE', p_qty, CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [rs00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_rs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_rs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_rs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_rs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_rs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_rs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_rs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_rs00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_rs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs00 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs00;
        loop_rs00: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs00 INTO v_so_rs00, v_cust_rs00, v_ctry_rs00, v_ccy_rs00, v_sub_rs00;
            IF v_done_rs00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_rs00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_rs00, v_sub_rs00);
            SET v_fx_rs00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_rs00, 1, v_sub_rs00 + v_tax_rs00);
            SET v_acc_rs00 = v_acc_rs00 + v_fx_rs00;
            SET v_cnt_rs00 = v_cnt_rs00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs00;
        IF v_cnt_rs00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_rs00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_rs00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [rs01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rs01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_rs01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_rs01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_rs01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_rs01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs01 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs01;
        loop_rs01: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs01 INTO v_pid_rs01, v_oh_rs01, v_cost_rs01;
            IF v_done_rs01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_rs01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_rs01);
            SET v_val_rs01 = v_val_rs01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_rs01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_rs01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_rs01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_rs01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_rs01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [rs02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_rs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_rs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_rs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_rs02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_rs02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_rs02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs02 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs02;
        loop_rs02: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs02 INTO v_prod_rs02, v_rm_rs02, v_supp_rs02, v_qper_rs02, v_uc_rs02;
            IF v_done_rs02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_rs02 = v_roll_rs02 + COALESCE(v_qper_rs02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_rs02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_rs02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [rs03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_rs03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_rs03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_rs03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_rs03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs03 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs03;
        loop_rs03: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs03 INTO v_acct_rs03, v_deb_rs03, v_cred_rs03;
            IF v_done_rs03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs03; END IF;
            SET v_net_rs03 = v_net_rs03 + (v_deb_rs03 - v_cred_rs03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_rs03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [rs04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rs04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_rs04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_rs04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_rs04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_rs04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs04 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs04;
        loop_rs04: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs04 INTO v_pid_rs04, v_lp_rs04, v_pp_rs04;
            IF v_done_rs04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs04; END IF;
            SET v_var_rs04 = v_var_rs04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_rs04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_rs04,0));
            SET v_marg_rs04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_rs04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_rs04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [rs05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_rs05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_rs05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_rs05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_rs05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs05 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs05;
        loop_rs05: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs05 INTO v_inv_rs05, v_amt_rs05, v_alloc_rs05;
            IF v_done_rs05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs05; END IF;
            SET v_open_rs05 = v_open_rs05 + (v_amt_rs05 - v_alloc_rs05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_rs05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [rs06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_rs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_rs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_rs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs06 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs06;
        loop_rs06: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs06 INTO v_dept_rs06, v_lvl_rs06, v_head_rs06;
            IF v_done_rs06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_rs06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_rs06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [rs07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_rs07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_rs07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_rs07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_rs07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs07 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs07;
        loop_rs07: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs07 INTO v_sh_rs07, v_car_rs07, v_qty_rs07;
            IF v_done_rs07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_rs07 = v_tot_rs07 + v_qty_rs07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_rs07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [rs08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_rs08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_rs08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_rs08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_rs08 FROM v_sql_rs08;
        EXECUTE st_rs08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_rs08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_rs08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [rs09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_rs09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_rs09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_rs09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_rs09 FROM v_sql_rs09;
        EXECUTE st_rs09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_rs09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_rs09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [rs10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_rs10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_rs10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_rs10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_rs10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [rs11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_rs11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_rs11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_rs11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_rs11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [rs12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_rs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_rs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_rs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_rs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_rs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_rs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_rs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_rs12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_rs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs12 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs12;
        loop_rs12: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs12 INTO v_so_rs12, v_cust_rs12, v_ctry_rs12, v_ccy_rs12, v_sub_rs12;
            IF v_done_rs12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_rs12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_rs12, v_sub_rs12);
            SET v_fx_rs12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_rs12, 1, v_sub_rs12 + v_tax_rs12);
            SET v_acc_rs12 = v_acc_rs12 + v_fx_rs12;
            SET v_cnt_rs12 = v_cnt_rs12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs12;
        IF v_cnt_rs12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_rs12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_rs12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [rs13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rs13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_rs13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_rs13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_rs13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_rs13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs13 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs13;
        loop_rs13: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs13 INTO v_pid_rs13, v_oh_rs13, v_cost_rs13;
            IF v_done_rs13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_rs13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_rs13);
            SET v_val_rs13 = v_val_rs13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_rs13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_rs13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_rs13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_rs13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_rs13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [rs14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_rs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_rs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_rs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_rs14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_rs14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_rs14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs14 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs14;
        loop_rs14: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs14 INTO v_prod_rs14, v_rm_rs14, v_supp_rs14, v_qper_rs14, v_uc_rs14;
            IF v_done_rs14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_rs14 = v_roll_rs14 + COALESCE(v_qper_rs14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_rs14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_rs14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [rs15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_rs15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_rs15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_rs15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_rs15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rs15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rs15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rs15 = 1;
        OPEN c_rs15;
        loop_rs15: LOOP
            FETCH c_rs15 INTO v_acct_rs15, v_deb_rs15, v_cred_rs15;
            IF v_done_rs15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rs15; END IF;
            SET v_net_rs15 = v_net_rs15 + (v_deb_rs15 - v_cred_rs15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rs15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_rs15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_REPLENISH_STOCK(p_product_id, p_qty, p_so_id);
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('INVENTORY', COALESCE(v_inv_id,0), 'RESERVE',
        'avail=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_avail)));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_ALLOCATE_INVENTORY:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_ALLOCATE_INVENTORY(IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_pid INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_qty DECIMAL(16,4);
    DECLARE v_onhand DECIMAL(16,4);
    DECLARE done INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE c_lines CURSOR FOR
        SELECT sol.product_id, sol.qty
        FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE sol
        JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = sol.product_id
        JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
        WHERE sol.so_id = p_so_id;
    DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = 1;
    OPEN c_lines;
    alloc_loop: LOOP
        FETCH c_lines INTO v_pid, v_qty;
        IF done = 1 THEN LEAVE alloc_loop; END IF;
        SET v_onhand = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid);
        CALL ERP_USP_RESERVE_STOCK(v_pid, v_qty, p_so_id);
    END LOOP;
    CLOSE c_lines;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [al00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_al00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_al00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_al00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_al00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_al00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_al00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_al00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_al00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_al00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al00 = 1;
        OPEN c_al00;
        loop_al00: LOOP
            FETCH c_al00 INTO v_so_al00, v_cust_al00, v_ctry_al00, v_ccy_al00, v_sub_al00;
            IF v_done_al00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_al00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_al00, v_sub_al00);
            SET v_fx_al00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_al00, 1, v_sub_al00 + v_tax_al00);
            SET v_acc_al00 = v_acc_al00 + v_fx_al00;
            SET v_cnt_al00 = v_cnt_al00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al00;
        IF v_cnt_al00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_al00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_al00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [al01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_al01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_al01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_al01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_al01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_al01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al01 = 1;
        OPEN c_al01;
        loop_al01: LOOP
            FETCH c_al01 INTO v_pid_al01, v_oh_al01, v_cost_al01;
            IF v_done_al01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_al01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_al01);
            SET v_val_al01 = v_val_al01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_al01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_al01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_al01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_al01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_al01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [al02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_al02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_al02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_al02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_al02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_al02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_al02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al02 = 1;
        OPEN c_al02;
        loop_al02: LOOP
            FETCH c_al02 INTO v_prod_al02, v_rm_al02, v_supp_al02, v_qper_al02, v_uc_al02;
            IF v_done_al02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_al02 = v_roll_al02 + COALESCE(v_qper_al02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_al02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_al02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [al03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_al03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_al03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_al03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_al03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al03 = 1;
        OPEN c_al03;
        loop_al03: LOOP
            FETCH c_al03 INTO v_acct_al03, v_deb_al03, v_cred_al03;
            IF v_done_al03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al03; END IF;
            SET v_net_al03 = v_net_al03 + (v_deb_al03 - v_cred_al03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_al03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [al04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_al04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_al04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_al04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_al04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_al04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al04 = 1;
        OPEN c_al04;
        loop_al04: LOOP
            FETCH c_al04 INTO v_pid_al04, v_lp_al04, v_pp_al04;
            IF v_done_al04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al04; END IF;
            SET v_var_al04 = v_var_al04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_al04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_al04,0));
            SET v_marg_al04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_al04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_al04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [al05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_al05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_al05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_al05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_al05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al05 = 1;
        OPEN c_al05;
        loop_al05: LOOP
            FETCH c_al05 INTO v_inv_al05, v_amt_al05, v_alloc_al05;
            IF v_done_al05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al05; END IF;
            SET v_open_al05 = v_open_al05 + (v_amt_al05 - v_alloc_al05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_al05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [al06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_al06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_al06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_al06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al06 = 1;
        OPEN c_al06;
        loop_al06: LOOP
            FETCH c_al06 INTO v_dept_al06, v_lvl_al06, v_head_al06;
            IF v_done_al06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_al06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_al06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [al07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_al07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_al07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_al07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_al07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al07 = 1;
        OPEN c_al07;
        loop_al07: LOOP
            FETCH c_al07 INTO v_sh_al07, v_car_al07, v_qty_al07;
            IF v_done_al07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_al07 = v_tot_al07 + v_qty_al07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_al07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [al08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_al08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_al08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_al08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_al08 FROM v_sql_al08;
        EXECUTE st_al08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_al08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_al08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [al09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_al09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_al09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_al09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_al09 FROM v_sql_al09;
        EXECUTE st_al09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_al09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_al09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [al10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_al10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_al10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_al10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_al10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [al11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_al11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_al11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_al11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_al11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [al12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_al12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_al12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_al12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_al12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_al12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_al12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_al12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_al12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_al12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al12 = 1;
        OPEN c_al12;
        loop_al12: LOOP
            FETCH c_al12 INTO v_so_al12, v_cust_al12, v_ctry_al12, v_ccy_al12, v_sub_al12;
            IF v_done_al12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_al12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_al12, v_sub_al12);
            SET v_fx_al12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_al12, 1, v_sub_al12 + v_tax_al12);
            SET v_acc_al12 = v_acc_al12 + v_fx_al12;
            SET v_cnt_al12 = v_cnt_al12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al12;
        IF v_cnt_al12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_al12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_al12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [al13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_al13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_al13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_al13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_al13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_al13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al13 = 1;
        OPEN c_al13;
        loop_al13: LOOP
            FETCH c_al13 INTO v_pid_al13, v_oh_al13, v_cost_al13;
            IF v_done_al13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_al13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_al13);
            SET v_val_al13 = v_val_al13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_al13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_al13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_al13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_al13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_al13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [al14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_al14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_al14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_al14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_al14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_al14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_al14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al14 = 1;
        OPEN c_al14;
        loop_al14: LOOP
            FETCH c_al14 INTO v_prod_al14, v_rm_al14, v_supp_al14, v_qper_al14, v_uc_al14;
            IF v_done_al14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_al14 = v_roll_al14 + COALESCE(v_qper_al14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_al14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_al14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [al15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_al15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_al15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_al15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_al15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_al15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_al15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_al15 = 1;
        OPEN c_al15;
        loop_al15: LOOP
            FETCH c_al15 INTO v_acct_al15, v_deb_al15, v_cred_al15;
            IF v_done_al15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_al15; END IF;
            SET v_net_al15 = v_net_al15 + (v_deb_al15 - v_cred_al15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_al15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_al15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('SALES_ORDER', p_so_id, 'ALLOCATE', 'inventory allocated');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_PROCESS_ORDER:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_PROCESS_ORDER(IN p_so_id INTEGER)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_tier VARCHAR(10);
    DECLARE v_cust INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_pid INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_qty DECIMAL(16,4);
    DECLARE v_price DECIMAL(14,2);
    DECLARE v_disc DECIMAL(6,4);
    DECLARE v_lineid INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_subtotal DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_margin DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_bal DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE v_ok INTEGER DEFAULT 1;
    DECLARE done INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE c_sol CURSOR FOR
        SELECT sol.id, sol.product_id, sol.qty, sol.unit_price
        FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE sol
        JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = sol.product_id
        WHERE sol.so_id = p_so_id;
    DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = 1;
    SELECT so.customer_id, c.tier INTO v_cust, v_tier
    FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
    WHERE so.id = p_so_id;
    OPEN c_sol;
    price_loop: LOOP
        FETCH c_sol INTO v_lineid, v_pid, v_qty, v_price;
        IF done = 1 THEN LEAVE price_loop; END IF;
        SET v_disc = ERP_FN_DISCOUNT_PCT(v_tier, CAST(v_qty AS INTEGER));
        SET v_margin = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid);
        UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE
        SET discount_pct = v_disc,
            line_total = ERP_FN_LINE_TOTAL(v_qty, v_price, v_disc)
        WHERE id = v_lineid;
    END LOOP;
    CLOSE c_sol;
    SET v_subtotal = ERP_FN_ORDER_SUBTOTAL(p_so_id);
    SET v_bal = ERP_FN_CUSTOMER_BALANCE(v_cust);
    SET v_ok = ERP_FN_CREDIT_OK(v_cust, v_subtotal);
    IF v_ok = 1 THEN
        UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = 'CONFIRMED', subtotal = v_subtotal WHERE id = p_so_id;
        CALL ERP_USP_ALLOCATE_INVENTORY(p_so_id);
    ELSE
        UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = 'HOLD_CREDIT' WHERE id = p_so_id;
        CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('SALES_ORDER', p_so_id, 'HOLD', 'Credit exceeded');
    END IF;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [po00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_po00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_po00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_po00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_po00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_po00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_po00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_po00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_po00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_po00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po00 = 1;
        OPEN c_po00;
        loop_po00: LOOP
            FETCH c_po00 INTO v_so_po00, v_cust_po00, v_ctry_po00, v_ccy_po00, v_sub_po00;
            IF v_done_po00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_po00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_po00, v_sub_po00);
            SET v_fx_po00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_po00, 1, v_sub_po00 + v_tax_po00);
            SET v_acc_po00 = v_acc_po00 + v_fx_po00;
            SET v_cnt_po00 = v_cnt_po00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po00;
        IF v_cnt_po00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_po00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_po00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [po01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_po01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_po01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_po01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_po01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_po01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po01 = 1;
        OPEN c_po01;
        loop_po01: LOOP
            FETCH c_po01 INTO v_pid_po01, v_oh_po01, v_cost_po01;
            IF v_done_po01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_po01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_po01);
            SET v_val_po01 = v_val_po01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_po01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_po01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_po01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_po01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_po01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [po02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_po02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_po02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_po02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_po02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_po02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_po02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po02 = 1;
        OPEN c_po02;
        loop_po02: LOOP
            FETCH c_po02 INTO v_prod_po02, v_rm_po02, v_supp_po02, v_qper_po02, v_uc_po02;
            IF v_done_po02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_po02 = v_roll_po02 + COALESCE(v_qper_po02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_po02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_po02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [po03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_po03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_po03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_po03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_po03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po03 = 1;
        OPEN c_po03;
        loop_po03: LOOP
            FETCH c_po03 INTO v_acct_po03, v_deb_po03, v_cred_po03;
            IF v_done_po03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po03; END IF;
            SET v_net_po03 = v_net_po03 + (v_deb_po03 - v_cred_po03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_po03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [po04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_po04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_po04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_po04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_po04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_po04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po04 = 1;
        OPEN c_po04;
        loop_po04: LOOP
            FETCH c_po04 INTO v_pid_po04, v_lp_po04, v_pp_po04;
            IF v_done_po04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po04; END IF;
            SET v_var_po04 = v_var_po04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_po04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_po04,0));
            SET v_marg_po04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_po04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_po04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [po05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_po05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_po05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_po05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_po05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po05 = 1;
        OPEN c_po05;
        loop_po05: LOOP
            FETCH c_po05 INTO v_inv_po05, v_amt_po05, v_alloc_po05;
            IF v_done_po05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po05; END IF;
            SET v_open_po05 = v_open_po05 + (v_amt_po05 - v_alloc_po05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_po05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [po06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_po06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_po06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_po06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po06 = 1;
        OPEN c_po06;
        loop_po06: LOOP
            FETCH c_po06 INTO v_dept_po06, v_lvl_po06, v_head_po06;
            IF v_done_po06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_po06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_po06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [po07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_po07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_po07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_po07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_po07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po07 = 1;
        OPEN c_po07;
        loop_po07: LOOP
            FETCH c_po07 INTO v_sh_po07, v_car_po07, v_qty_po07;
            IF v_done_po07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_po07 = v_tot_po07 + v_qty_po07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_po07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [po08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_po08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_po08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_po08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_po08 FROM v_sql_po08;
        EXECUTE st_po08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_po08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_po08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [po09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_po09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_po09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_po09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_po09 FROM v_sql_po09;
        EXECUTE st_po09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_po09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_po09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [po10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_po10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_po10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_po10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_po10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [po11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_po11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_po11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_po11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_po11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [po12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_po12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_po12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_po12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_po12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_po12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_po12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_po12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_po12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_po12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po12 = 1;
        OPEN c_po12;
        loop_po12: LOOP
            FETCH c_po12 INTO v_so_po12, v_cust_po12, v_ctry_po12, v_ccy_po12, v_sub_po12;
            IF v_done_po12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_po12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_po12, v_sub_po12);
            SET v_fx_po12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_po12, 1, v_sub_po12 + v_tax_po12);
            SET v_acc_po12 = v_acc_po12 + v_fx_po12;
            SET v_cnt_po12 = v_cnt_po12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po12;
        IF v_cnt_po12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_po12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_po12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [po13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_po13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_po13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_po13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_po13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_po13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po13 = 1;
        OPEN c_po13;
        loop_po13: LOOP
            FETCH c_po13 INTO v_pid_po13, v_oh_po13, v_cost_po13;
            IF v_done_po13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_po13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_po13);
            SET v_val_po13 = v_val_po13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_po13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_po13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_po13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_po13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_po13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [po14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_po14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_po14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_po14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_po14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_po14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_po14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_po14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_po14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_po14 = 1;
        OPEN c_po14;
        loop_po14: LOOP
            FETCH c_po14 INTO v_prod_po14, v_rm_po14, v_supp_po14, v_qper_po14, v_uc_po14;
            IF v_done_po14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_po14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_po14 = v_roll_po14 + COALESCE(v_qper_po14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_po14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_po14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_po14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('SALES_ORDER', p_so_id, 'PROCESS',
        'subtotal=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_subtotal)));
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_PROCESS_ORDERS:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_PROCESS_ORDERS()
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_so INTEGER;
    DECLARE v_doc INTEGER;
    DECLARE done INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    DECLARE c_orders CURSOR FOR SELECT id FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status = 'NEW' ORDER BY id;
    DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = 1;
    OPEN c_orders;
    order_loop: LOOP
        FETCH c_orders INTO v_so;
        IF done = 1 THEN LEAVE order_loop; END IF;
        SET v_doc = NEXT VALUE FOR ERP_ORDER_SEQ;
        UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET doc_no = v_doc WHERE id = v_so;
        CALL ERP_USP_PROCESS_ORDER(v_so);
    END LOOP;
    CLOSE c_orders;
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [os00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_os00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_os00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_os00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_os00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_os00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_os00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_os00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_os00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_os00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os00 = 1;
        OPEN c_os00;
        loop_os00: LOOP
            FETCH c_os00 INTO v_so_os00, v_cust_os00, v_ctry_os00, v_ccy_os00, v_sub_os00;
            IF v_done_os00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_os00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_os00, v_sub_os00);
            SET v_fx_os00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_os00, 1, v_sub_os00 + v_tax_os00);
            SET v_acc_os00 = v_acc_os00 + v_fx_os00;
            SET v_cnt_os00 = v_cnt_os00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os00;
        IF v_cnt_os00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_os00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_os00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [os01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_os01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_os01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_os01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_os01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_os01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os01 = 1;
        OPEN c_os01;
        loop_os01: LOOP
            FETCH c_os01 INTO v_pid_os01, v_oh_os01, v_cost_os01;
            IF v_done_os01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_os01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_os01);
            SET v_val_os01 = v_val_os01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_os01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_os01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_os01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_os01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_os01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [os02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_os02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_os02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_os02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_os02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_os02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_os02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os02 = 1;
        OPEN c_os02;
        loop_os02: LOOP
            FETCH c_os02 INTO v_prod_os02, v_rm_os02, v_supp_os02, v_qper_os02, v_uc_os02;
            IF v_done_os02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_os02 = v_roll_os02 + COALESCE(v_qper_os02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_os02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_os02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [os03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_os03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_os03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_os03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_os03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os03 = 1;
        OPEN c_os03;
        loop_os03: LOOP
            FETCH c_os03 INTO v_acct_os03, v_deb_os03, v_cred_os03;
            IF v_done_os03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os03; END IF;
            SET v_net_os03 = v_net_os03 + (v_deb_os03 - v_cred_os03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_os03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [os04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_os04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_os04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_os04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_os04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_os04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os04 = 1;
        OPEN c_os04;
        loop_os04: LOOP
            FETCH c_os04 INTO v_pid_os04, v_lp_os04, v_pp_os04;
            IF v_done_os04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os04; END IF;
            SET v_var_os04 = v_var_os04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_os04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_os04,0));
            SET v_marg_os04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_os04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_os04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [os05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_os05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_os05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_os05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_os05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os05 = 1;
        OPEN c_os05;
        loop_os05: LOOP
            FETCH c_os05 INTO v_inv_os05, v_amt_os05, v_alloc_os05;
            IF v_done_os05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os05; END IF;
            SET v_open_os05 = v_open_os05 + (v_amt_os05 - v_alloc_os05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_os05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [os06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_os06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_os06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_os06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os06 = 1;
        OPEN c_os06;
        loop_os06: LOOP
            FETCH c_os06 INTO v_dept_os06, v_lvl_os06, v_head_os06;
            IF v_done_os06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_os06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_os06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [os07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_os07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_os07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_os07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_os07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os07 = 1;
        OPEN c_os07;
        loop_os07: LOOP
            FETCH c_os07 INTO v_sh_os07, v_car_os07, v_qty_os07;
            IF v_done_os07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_os07 = v_tot_os07 + v_qty_os07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_os07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [os08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_os08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_os08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_os08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_os08 FROM v_sql_os08;
        EXECUTE st_os08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_os08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_os08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [os09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_os09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_os09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_os09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_os09 FROM v_sql_os09;
        EXECUTE st_os09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_os09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_os09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [os10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_os10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_os10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_os10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_os10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [os11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_os11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_os11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_os11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_os11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [os12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_os12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_os12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_os12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_os12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_os12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_os12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_os12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_os12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_os12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os12 = 1;
        OPEN c_os12;
        loop_os12: LOOP
            FETCH c_os12 INTO v_so_os12, v_cust_os12, v_ctry_os12, v_ccy_os12, v_sub_os12;
            IF v_done_os12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_os12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_os12, v_sub_os12);
            SET v_fx_os12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_os12, 1, v_sub_os12 + v_tax_os12);
            SET v_acc_os12 = v_acc_os12 + v_fx_os12;
            SET v_cnt_os12 = v_cnt_os12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os12;
        IF v_cnt_os12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_os12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_os12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [os13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_os13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_os13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_os13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_os13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_os13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os13 = 1;
        OPEN c_os13;
        loop_os13: LOOP
            FETCH c_os13 INTO v_pid_os13, v_oh_os13, v_cost_os13;
            IF v_done_os13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_os13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_os13);
            SET v_val_os13 = v_val_os13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_os13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_os13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_os13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_os13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_os13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [os14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_os14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_os14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_os14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_os14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_os14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_os14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os14 = 1;
        OPEN c_os14;
        loop_os14: LOOP
            FETCH c_os14 INTO v_prod_os14, v_rm_os14, v_supp_os14, v_qper_os14, v_uc_os14;
            IF v_done_os14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_os14 = v_roll_os14 + COALESCE(v_qper_os14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_os14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
