        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_os14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [os15]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_os15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_os15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_os15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_os15 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_os15 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_os15 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_os15 = 1;
        OPEN c_os15;
        loop_os15: LOOP
            FETCH c_os15 INTO v_acct_os15, v_deb_os15, v_cred_os15;
            IF v_done_os15 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_os15; END IF;
            SET v_net_os15 = v_net_os15 + (v_deb_os15 - v_cred_os15);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_os15;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_os15 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('CYCLE', 0, 'PROCESS_ORDERS', 'all new orders processed');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_PROCESS_DAY:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_PROCESS_DAY(IN p_run_date DATE)
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status = 'NEW';
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('CYCLE', 0, 'START', 'Processing ' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_count)) || ' orders');
    CALL ERP_USP_PROCESS_ORDERS();
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [pd00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_pd00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_pd00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_pd00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_pd00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_pd00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_pd00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_pd00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_pd00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_pd00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd00 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd00;
        loop_pd00: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd00 INTO v_so_pd00, v_cust_pd00, v_ctry_pd00, v_ccy_pd00, v_sub_pd00;
            IF v_done_pd00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_pd00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_pd00, v_sub_pd00);
            SET v_fx_pd00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_pd00, 1, v_sub_pd00 + v_tax_pd00);
            SET v_acc_pd00 = v_acc_pd00 + v_fx_pd00;
            SET v_cnt_pd00 = v_cnt_pd00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd00;
        IF v_cnt_pd00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_pd00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_pd00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [pd01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pd01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_pd01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_pd01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_pd01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_pd01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd01 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd01;
        loop_pd01: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd01 INTO v_pid_pd01, v_oh_pd01, v_cost_pd01;
            IF v_done_pd01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_pd01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_pd01);
            SET v_val_pd01 = v_val_pd01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_pd01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_pd01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_pd01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_pd01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_pd01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [pd02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_pd02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_pd02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_pd02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_pd02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_pd02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_pd02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd02 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd02;
        loop_pd02: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd02 INTO v_prod_pd02, v_rm_pd02, v_supp_pd02, v_qper_pd02, v_uc_pd02;
            IF v_done_pd02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_pd02 = v_roll_pd02 + COALESCE(v_qper_pd02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_pd02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_pd02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [pd03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_pd03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_pd03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_pd03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_pd03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd03 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd03;
        loop_pd03: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd03 INTO v_acct_pd03, v_deb_pd03, v_cred_pd03;
            IF v_done_pd03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd03; END IF;
            SET v_net_pd03 = v_net_pd03 + (v_deb_pd03 - v_cred_pd03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_pd03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [pd04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pd04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_pd04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_pd04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_pd04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_pd04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd04 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd04;
        loop_pd04: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd04 INTO v_pid_pd04, v_lp_pd04, v_pp_pd04;
            IF v_done_pd04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd04; END IF;
            SET v_var_pd04 = v_var_pd04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_pd04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_pd04,0));
            SET v_marg_pd04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_pd04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_pd04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [pd05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_pd05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_pd05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_pd05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_pd05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd05 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd05;
        loop_pd05: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd05 INTO v_inv_pd05, v_amt_pd05, v_alloc_pd05;
            IF v_done_pd05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd05; END IF;
            SET v_open_pd05 = v_open_pd05 + (v_amt_pd05 - v_alloc_pd05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_pd05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [pd06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_pd06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_pd06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_pd06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd06 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd06;
        loop_pd06: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd06 INTO v_dept_pd06, v_lvl_pd06, v_head_pd06;
            IF v_done_pd06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_pd06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_pd06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [pd07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_pd07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_pd07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_pd07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_pd07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd07 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd07;
        loop_pd07: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd07 INTO v_sh_pd07, v_car_pd07, v_qty_pd07;
            IF v_done_pd07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_pd07 = v_tot_pd07 + v_qty_pd07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_pd07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [pd08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_pd08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_pd08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_pd08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_pd08 FROM v_sql_pd08;
        EXECUTE st_pd08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_pd08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_pd08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [pd09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_pd09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_pd09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_pd09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_pd09 FROM v_sql_pd09;
        EXECUTE st_pd09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_pd09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_pd09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [pd10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_pd10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_pd10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_pd10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_pd10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [pd11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_pd11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_pd11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_pd11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_pd11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [pd12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_pd12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_pd12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_pd12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_pd12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_pd12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_pd12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_pd12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_pd12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_pd12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd12 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd12;
        loop_pd12: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd12 INTO v_so_pd12, v_cust_pd12, v_ctry_pd12, v_ccy_pd12, v_sub_pd12;
            IF v_done_pd12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_pd12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_pd12, v_sub_pd12);
            SET v_fx_pd12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_pd12, 1, v_sub_pd12 + v_tax_pd12);
            SET v_acc_pd12 = v_acc_pd12 + v_fx_pd12;
            SET v_cnt_pd12 = v_cnt_pd12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd12;
        IF v_cnt_pd12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_pd12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_pd12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [pd13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_pd13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_pd13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_pd13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_pd13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_pd13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd13 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd13;
        loop_pd13: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd13 INTO v_pid_pd13, v_oh_pd13, v_cost_pd13;
            IF v_done_pd13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_pd13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_pd13);
            SET v_val_pd13 = v_val_pd13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_pd13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_pd13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_pd13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_pd13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_pd13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [pd14]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_pd14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_pd14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_pd14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_pd14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_pd14 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_pd14 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_pd14 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_pd14 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_pd14 = 1;
        OPEN c_pd14;
        loop_pd14: LOOP
            FETCH c_pd14 INTO v_prod_pd14, v_rm_pd14, v_supp_pd14, v_qper_pd14, v_uc_pd14;
            IF v_done_pd14 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_pd14; END IF;
            SET v_roll_pd14 = v_roll_pd14 + COALESCE(v_qper_pd14,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_pd14,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_pd14;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_pd14 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('CYCLE', p_run_date - p_run_date, 'PROCESS_DAY', 'day complete');
END
===END===
===OBJECT:POSTGRESINST1.ERP_USP_RUN_CYCLE:PROCEDURE===
CREATE PROCEDURE ERP_USP_RUN_CYCLE()
LANGUAGE SQL
BEGIN
    CALL ERP_USP_AUDIT('CYCLE', 0, 'RUN', 'Cycle invoked');
    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [rc00]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_rc00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_rc00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_rc00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_rc00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_rc00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_rc00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_rc00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_rc00 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_rc00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc00 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc00 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc00 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc00;
        loop_rc00: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc00 INTO v_so_rc00, v_cust_rc00, v_ctry_rc00, v_ccy_rc00, v_sub_rc00;
            IF v_done_rc00 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc00; END IF;
            SET v_tax_rc00 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_rc00, v_sub_rc00);
            SET v_fx_rc00 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_rc00, 1, v_sub_rc00 + v_tax_rc00);
            SET v_acc_rc00 = v_acc_rc00 + v_fx_rc00;
            SET v_cnt_rc00 = v_cnt_rc00 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc00;
        IF v_cnt_rc00 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_rc00, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_rc00 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [rc01]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rc01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_rc01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_rc01 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_rc01 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_rc01 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc01 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc01 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc01 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc01;
        loop_rc01: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc01 INTO v_pid_rc01, v_oh_rc01, v_cost_rc01;
            IF v_done_rc01 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc01; END IF;
            SET v_avail_rc01 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_rc01);
            SET v_val_rc01 = v_val_rc01 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_rc01,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_rc01,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_rc01, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_rc01;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc01;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_rc01 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- BOM explosion supplier cost roll-up [rc02]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_prod_rc02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_rm_rc02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_supp_rc02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qper_rc02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_uc_rc02 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_roll_rc02 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc02 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc02 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT b.product_id, b.raw_material_id, rm.supplier_id, b.qty_per, rm.unit_cost
            FROM ERP_BOM b
            JOIN ERP_RAW_MATERIAL rm ON rm.id = b.raw_material_id
            JOIN ERP_SUPPLIER sup ON sup.id = rm.supplier_id
            ORDER BY b.product_id, b.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc02 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc02;
        loop_rc02: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc02 INTO v_prod_rc02, v_rm_rc02, v_supp_rc02, v_qper_rc02, v_uc_rc02;
            IF v_done_rc02 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc02; END IF;
            SET v_roll_rc02 = v_roll_rc02 + COALESCE(v_qper_rc02,0) * COALESCE(v_uc_rc02,0);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc02;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('BOM_COST', 0, 'ROLLUP', 'material_cost=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_roll_rc02 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- GL account reconciliation [rc03]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_acct_rc03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_deb_rc03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cred_rc03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_net_rc03 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc03 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc03 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT ga.id, COALESCE(SUM(ge.debit),0), COALESCE(SUM(ge.credit),0)
            FROM ERP_GL_ACCOUNT ga
            LEFT JOIN ERP_GL_ENTRY ge ON ge.account_id = ga.id
            GROUP BY ga.id
            ORDER BY ga.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc03 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc03;
        loop_rc03: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc03 INTO v_acct_rc03, v_deb_rc03, v_cred_rc03;
            IF v_done_rc03 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc03; END IF;
            SET v_net_rc03 = v_net_rc03 + (v_deb_rc03 - v_cred_rc03);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc03;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('GL_RECON', 0, 'RECON', 'net=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_net_rc03 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- price list vs list price variance [rc04]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rc04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_lp_rc04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_pp_rc04 DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_var_rc04 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_marg_rc04 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc04 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc04 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, p.list_price, pp.price
            FROM ERP_PRODUCT p
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_PRICE pp ON pp.product_id = p.id
            JOIN ERP_PRICE_LIST pl ON pl.id = pp.price_list_id
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT_CATEGORY pc ON pc.id = p.category_id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc04 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc04;
        loop_rc04: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc04 INTO v_pid_rc04, v_lp_rc04, v_pp_rc04;
            IF v_done_rc04 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc04; END IF;
            SET v_var_rc04 = v_var_rc04 + (COALESCE(v_lp_rc04,0) - COALESCE(v_pp_rc04,0));
            SET v_marg_rc04 = ERP_FN_MARGIN(v_pid_rc04);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc04;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('PRICE_VAR', 0, 'SCAN', 'variance=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_var_rc04 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- payment allocation coverage [rc05]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_inv_rc05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_amt_rc05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_alloc_rc05 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_open_rc05 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc05 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc05 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT i.id, COALESCE(i.amount,0), COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount),0)
            FROM ERP_INVOICE i
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT_ALLOC pa ON pa.invoice_id = i.id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_PAYMENT pay ON pay.id = pa.payment_id
            GROUP BY i.id, i.amount
            ORDER BY i.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc05 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc05;
        loop_rc05: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc05 INTO v_inv_rc05, v_amt_rc05, v_alloc_rc05;
            IF v_done_rc05 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc05; END IF;
            SET v_open_rc05 = v_open_rc05 + (v_amt_rc05 - v_alloc_rc05);
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc05;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('AR_OPEN', 0, 'SCAN', 'open_ar=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_open_rc05 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- department staffing (recursive CTE, CAST'd literals) [rc06]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_lvl_rc06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_dept_rc06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_head_rc06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc06 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc06 CURSOR FOR
            WITH dh (dept_id, parent_id, lvl) AS (
                SELECT d.id, d.parent_id, CAST(1 AS INTEGER)
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d WHERE d.parent_id IS NULL
                UNION ALL
                SELECT d2.id, d2.parent_id, dh.lvl + 1
                FROM ERP_DEPARTMENT d2 JOIN dh ON d2.parent_id = dh.dept_id
                WHERE dh.lvl < 10
            )
            SELECT dh.dept_id, dh.lvl, COUNT(e.id)
            FROM dh LEFT JOIN ERP_EMPLOYEE e ON e.dept_id = dh.dept_id
            GROUP BY dh.dept_id, dh.lvl
            ORDER BY dh.dept_id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc06 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc06;
        loop_rc06: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc06 INTO v_dept_rc06, v_lvl_rc06, v_head_rc06;
            IF v_done_rc06 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc06; END IF;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc06;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DEPT_HIER', v_dept_rc06, 'SCAN', 'depth=' || RTRIM(CHAR(v_lvl_rc06)), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- shipment fulfilment scan [rc07]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sh_rc07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_car_rc07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_qty_rc07 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tot_rc07 DECIMAL(18,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc07 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc07 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT sh.id, sh.carrier_id, COALESCE(SUM(sl.qty_shipped),0)
            FROM ERP_SHIPMENT sh
            JOIN ERP_CARRIER ca ON ca.id = sh.carrier_id
            LEFT JOIN ERP_SHIPMENT_LINE sl ON sl.shipment_id = sh.id
            GROUP BY sh.id, sh.carrier_id
            ORDER BY sh.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc07 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc07;
        loop_rc07: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc07 INTO v_sh_rc07, v_car_rc07, v_qty_rc07;
            IF v_done_rc07 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc07; END IF;
            SET v_tot_rc07 = v_tot_rc07 + v_qty_rc07;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc07;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('SHIP_SCAN', 0, 'SCAN', 'units=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_tot_rc07 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [rc08]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_rc08 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_rc08 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_rc08 = 'UPDATE ERP_SALES_ORDER SET status = status WHERE status IS NOT NULL';
        PREPARE st_rc08 FROM v_sql_rc08;
        EXECUTE st_rc08;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_rc08 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER WHERE status IS NOT NULL;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_rc08, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_SALES_ORDER', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- dynamic SQL maintenance pass [rc09]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_sql_rc09 VARCHAR(1000);
        DECLARE v_n_rc09 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SET v_sql_rc09 = 'UPDATE ERP_INVENTORY SET reserved = reserved WHERE reserved >= 0';
        PREPARE st_rc09 FROM v_sql_rc09;
        EXECUTE st_rc09;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_n_rc09 FROM ERP_INVENTORY WHERE reserved >= 0;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('DYN_SQL', v_n_rc09, 'MAINT', 'tbl=ERP_INVENTORY', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [rc10]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_rc10 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_rc10 FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_rc10' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_rc10)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_SALES_ORDER_LINE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- MERGE upsert of run statistics [rc11]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_key_rc11 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*),0) INTO v_key_rc11 FROM ERP_INVOICE;
        MERGE INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG AS tgt
        USING (VALUES (CAST('STAT_rc11' AS VARCHAR(40)), v_key_rc11)) AS src(ent, cnt)
        ON tgt.entity = src.ent AND tgt.entity_id = src.cnt
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
            INSERT (entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES (src.ent, src.cnt, 'MERGE', 'rows in ERP_INVOICE', CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    -- order/customer/country/currency revenue scan [rc12]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_so_rc12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cust_rc12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ctry_rc12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_ccy_rc12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_sub_rc12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_fx_rc12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_tax_rc12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_acc_rc12 DECIMAL(16,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cnt_rc12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc12 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc12 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT so.id, so.customer_id, c.country_id, so.ccy_id, so.subtotal
            FROM ERP_SALES_ORDER so
            JOIN ERP_CUSTOMER c ON c.id = so.customer_id
            JOIN ERP_COUNTRY ctry ON ctry.id = c.country_id
            JOIN ERP_CURRENCY cur ON cur.id = so.ccy_id
            WHERE so.subtotal >= 0
            ORDER BY so.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc12 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc12;
        loop_rc12: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc12 INTO v_so_rc12, v_cust_rc12, v_ctry_rc12, v_ccy_rc12, v_sub_rc12;
            IF v_done_rc12 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc12; END IF;
            SET v_tax_rc12 = ERP_FN_TAX(v_ctry_rc12, v_sub_rc12);
            SET v_fx_rc12 = ERP_FN_FX_CONVERT(v_ccy_rc12, 1, v_sub_rc12 + v_tax_rc12);
            SET v_acc_rc12 = v_acc_rc12 + v_fx_rc12;
            SET v_cnt_rc12 = v_cnt_rc12 + 1;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc12;
        IF v_cnt_rc12 > 0 THEN
            INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
            VALUES ('REV_SCAN', v_cnt_rc12, 'SCAN',
                    'usd_revenue=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_acc_rc12 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
        END IF;
    END;

    -- inventory valuation across product/location/warehouse [rc13]
    BEGIN
        DECLARE v_pid_rc13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_oh_rc13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_cost_rc13 DECIMAL(14,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_val_rc13 DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_avail_rc13 DECIMAL(16,4) DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE v_done_rc13 INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
        DECLARE c_rc13 CURSOR FOR
            SELECT p.id, SUM(i.on_hand), MAX(p.std_cost)
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i
            JOIN ERP_PRODUCT p ON p.id = i.product_id
            JOIN ERP_LOCATION l ON l.id = i.location_id
            JOIN ERP_WAREHOUSE w ON w.id = l.warehouse_id
            GROUP BY p.id
            ORDER BY p.id;
        DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done_rc13 = 1;
        OPEN c_rc13;
        loop_rc13: LOOP
            FETCH c_rc13 INTO v_pid_rc13, v_oh_rc13, v_cost_rc13;
            IF v_done_rc13 = 1 THEN LEAVE loop_rc13; END IF;
            SET v_avail_rc13 = ERP_FN_INVENTORY_ONHAND(v_pid_rc13);
            SET v_val_rc13 = v_val_rc13 + CAST(COALESCE(v_oh_rc13,0) * COALESCE(v_cost_rc13,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2));
            INSERT INTO ERP_INVENTORY_TXN(inventory_id, txn_type, qty, txn_ts)
            SELECT MIN(i2.id), 'VALUE', v_avail_rc13, CURRENT TIMESTAMP
            FROM ERP_INVENTORY i2 WHERE i2.product_id = v_pid_rc13;
        END LOOP;
        CLOSE c_rc13;
        INSERT INTO ERP_AUDIT_LOG(entity, entity_id, action, detail, logged_ts)
        VALUES ('INV_VAL', 0, 'VALUE', 'total=' || RTRIM(CHAR(CAST(v_val_rc13 AS INTEGER))), CURRENT TIMESTAMP);
    END;

    CALL ERP_USP_PROCESS_DAY(CURRENT DATE);
END
===END===
